**A-founder-2026-10-19e: Premium hover. A product card lifts 4 px, its photograph zooms 6 % and a
shadow fades in, over 500 ms; the name's line draws from the inline-start; a pill's colour fades
and its press settles (motion 6 of `system/motion.dc.html` and its Binding; A23 clause 11; A24
clause 9; AC-44 and T-48 amended; AC-6 as A24 clause 9 (c) reads it, unchanged and binding; no new
AC or T id).**
Status: approved in substance by the founder's ruling of 2026-10-19 (below). The founder confirms
this text when they approve the docs PR that carries it. The spec writer's readings are marked
*Reading* and can be overruled in one word.

**Ruling, verbatim.** The founder, 2026-10-19, in chat: "hover still doesn't have that animation
yet bro.. the interaction with the site doesn't feel premium". The founder then ruled "premium A":
the approved hover (motion 6) and a more premium version drawn side by side, both moving, before
anything is built. The designer drew both on `docs/design/system/motion-premium.dc.html` (draft
PR 282, branch `docs/design-premium-hover`). The founder's reply, 2026-10-19, in chat, verbatim:
"go with the premium proposed version and fix c". Its "go with the premium proposed version" picks
that sheet's right-hand column, "Premium (proposed)", with every value of its "Proposed values"
table and its "Proposed token". Its "fix c" is A-founder-2026-10-19f's (spec 003 §14). The reply
reached the spec writer through the orchestrator's work order.

**Trigger and evidence.**
- `docs/design/system/motion-premium.dc.html` on PR 282: the annotation's "Binding, both columns",
  "Proposed values", "Proposed token" and "For the implementer" rows, and the drawn states §1 to §4
  (the priority card, a coarse pointer, reduced motion and keyboard focus).
- What ships on `main` today: `src/app/globals.css`, the motion 6 block inside
  `prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference` and `(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)`
  (`a[data-fo-card-motion]` and its `img`: `transition-property: transform`, `--duration-base`,
  `--ease-standard`, `translateY(-2px)`, `scale(1.03)`); `src/modules/ui/shop/ProductCard.tsx`, the
  name's `decoration-1 underline-offset-[5px] group-hover:underline` and `data-fo-card-motion` on
  every card but the priority one; `src/modules/ui/primitives/Button.tsx`, each variant's
  `hover:bg-*-strong` (or `hover:bg-surface-raised`), which snaps, and
  `motion-safe:active:scale-[0.98]` at `--duration-fast`; `src/modules/ui/layout/noticeShell.ts`,
  the two notice actions, the same.
- `tests/e2e/motion.spec.ts` section 6b asserts `matrix(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, -2)` and
  `matrix(1.03, 0, 0, 1.03, 0, 0)` after a fixed 400 ms wait.

**Original.**
- `system/motion.dc.html`, motion 6 ("Card lift and photo zoom; button press", approved by the
  founder 2026-10-05), its Tokens row: "Card `translateY(-2px)`, photo `scale(1.03)` inside its
  clipped box, `--duration-base` 200 ms `--ease-standard`; press `scale(0.98)` for
  `--duration-fast`." The sheet's Binding holds UI motion to 120 to 320 ms from the duration tokens,
  with the bloom loop at 1,200 ms at most.
- A23 clause 11: transform and opacity only, CLS 0, a reduced-motion version of every motion, no
  animation delays the LCP element. A24 clause 9 approves the thirteen motions as drawn, adds
  `--duration-draw` and `--duration-bloom` (a) and restates reduced motion (c). AC-44 and T-48, as
  A24 clause 9 amends them, test that.

**Corrected.**
1. **Motion 6 now reads as the premium column.** Every value below is the sheet's, and binds:

   | Part | Rest | Hover (or press) | Duration and curve, in and out |
   |---|---|---|---|
   | (a) Card link, `a[data-fo-card-motion]` | `transform: none` | `translateY(-4px)` | `--duration-hover` 500 ms, `--ease-photo` `cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.7, 0.2, 1)` |
   | (b) Photograph, inside its clipped 4∶5 box | `transform: none` | `scale(1.06)` | the same as (a) |
   | (c) Shadow layer | `opacity: 0` | `opacity: 1` | the same as (a) |
   | (d) The name's line | `transform: scaleX(0)` | `scaleX(1)` | `--duration-slow` 320 ms, `--ease-emphasised` |
   | (e) Pill hover layer | `opacity: 0` | `opacity: 1` | `--duration-base` 200 ms, `--ease-standard` |
   | (f) Pill press | `transform: none` | `scale(0.98)` while pressed | in: `--duration-fast` 120 ms, `--ease-standard`; release: `--duration-base` 200 ms, `--ease-emphasised` |

   - **(c) The shadow layer** is one `aria-hidden` element per moving card: a sibling of the
     photograph's clipped ratio box, outside the clip and inside the link (so it lifts with the
     link), with the photo box's size and radius. It paints `box-shadow: var(--shadow-letter)`
     (`0 1px 1px` and `0 22px 44px -12px`, in `--color-shade`, ink at 12 %) at all times; only its
     `opacity` changes. `box-shadow` itself never transitions.
   - **(d) The name's line** is one `aria-hidden` element inside the card's heading, after the
     `<bdi>`: 1 px tall, `--color-ink`, 5 px under the baseline, as wide as the name. Its
     `transform-origin` is the inline-start: `0 50%`, and `100% 50%` under `[dir="rtl"]`. It
     retracts the same way on leave. A card that draws the line renders no `text-decoration`
     underline on its name: one line, never two. *Reading:* a name that wraps draws one line, under
     its last line box, as wide as the name's box; the sheet draws one-line names only.
   - **(e) The pill's layer** sits under the label (`::before` or an equivalent element) in the
     variant's hover colour today: `--color-accent-strong` for `primary` and `accent`,
     `--color-surface-raised` for `secondary`, `--color-danger-strong` for `danger`, and the same
     for the two notice actions in `layout/noticeShell.ts`. The variant's `background-color` no
     longer changes on hover. While pressed, the pill shows today's active colour
     (`--color-surface-muted` for `secondary`; the hover colour for the others), at once.
   - **(f) The press** applies on every pointer, touch included, as today.
   - Hover-out uses the same duration and curve as hover-in. No `will-change` is left on at rest.
     The build plays each motion once per hover; the sheet's loops are a canvas device.
2. **Where it applies.** Every product card that is not the priority card, wherever `ProductCard`
   renders it (TASK-277's widened row attaches motion 6 to every product card), and every pill:
   `Button`'s `primary`, `accent`, `secondary` and `danger` variants and the notice actions in
   `layout/noticeShell.ts`. **Not here:** the consent banner's answer pills
   (`ConsentBannerView.tsx`) and the checkout's pills (`src/modules/ui/checkout/styles.ts`) keep
   today's behaviour; matching them is a new row if the founder wants it (open item below).
3. **The token.** `--duration-hover: 500ms` lands in `src/app/globals.css` and
   `docs/design/system/tokens.css` together, in the same PR, exactly as A24 clause 9 (a) landed its
   two tokens.
4. **The 500 ms exception (amends the Binding of `system/motion.dc.html` and A23 clause 11).** UI
   motion stays between 120 and 320 ms from the duration tokens, with one more exception beside A24
   clause 9's `--duration-draw` and `--duration-bloom`: `--duration-hover`. It may be used only by
   the hover-in and hover-out transitions of a moving card's link, its photograph and its shadow
   layer, that is, only in rules whose selector contains `[data-fo-card-motion]`. It is never used
   by the name's line, a pill, anything that plays without a pointer's hover (a load, a scroll, a
   section reveal, a navigation, a dialog or sheet), anything that runs at first paint, or the LCP
   element and its ancestors.
5. **What holds, unchanged and binding.**
   - **Transform and opacity only** (AC-44). Nothing on the card or the pill transitions
     `box-shadow`, `background-color`, `text-decoration`, a size or a position.
   - **CLS 0.** The lift and the zoom are transforms; the photograph scales inside its clipped box;
     the shadow layer and the line are absolutely positioned inside boxes that exist at rest. Hover
     changes no layout.
   - **No motion on the priority card.** The priority card (the page's LCP photograph, including a
     card rendered with `priorityAt`) carries no `data-fo-card-motion`, no shadow layer and no line
     element, and it and its ancestors carry no transition (AC-44). Its name keeps today's
     `text-decoration` underline on hover, which is not a motion.
   - **Fine pointer only.** Every card and pill hover rule sits inside
     `(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)`. On a coarse pointer nothing lifts, zooms, casts a shadow
     or draws a line; the pill keeps its press (f).
   - **Reduced motion off.** Under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`: no lift, zoom or press, and the
     shadow layer stays at `opacity: 0`. The name's line rests at `scaleX(1)` with `opacity: 0` and,
     on hover, shows at `opacity: 1` at once, with no transition; the pill's layer shows at once. So
     no transform changes under reduce, and A24 clause 9 (c) holds as written.
   - **Keyboard focus** is unchanged: the shared focus ring around the card link or the pill; focus
     starts no motion.
   - CSS only: no JavaScript, no new dependency, no client byte (AC-25 unchanged).
6. **Design (the designer's, docs only, before TASK-277 builds these values).** PR 282 marks the
   premium column approved (founder, 2026-10-19) and the left column superseded; updates
   `system/motion.dc.html`'s motion 6 cell (Tokens, Reduced motion and Where rows) to the values of
   clause 1 and its Binding line to clause 4's exception; and marks its `docs/design/README.md` row
   answered. `docs/design/` stays the source of truth (CLAUDE.md), so the build matches PR 282's
   right column pixel for pixel.
7. **Unchanged.** Spec 008 AC-6 (the card's contents, as spec 008 §14 A-founder-2026-10-19b amends
   them); motions 1 to 5 and 7 to 13; A-founder-2026-10-19a; AC-24's LCP and script budgets.

**SEO.** Ranking inputs are untouched. The priority card, which holds the listing's LCP
photograph, gains nothing and keeps AC-44's guard, so LCP cannot move. Hover cannot happen before a
pointer arrives, so nothing here runs at first paint, and the 500 ms is confined to hover by
clause 4. CLS stays 0 (transforms and absolutely positioned layers). No client JavaScript is added;
INP is unaffected because hover and press are CSS. The server HTML gains two empty `aria-hidden`
elements per moving card and nothing a crawler reads: no text, link, URL, canonical, robots,
hreflang, sitemap or schema change. Rendering stays as `plan/01` §3 sets it.

**i18n.** No message key is added, changed or removed. The line draws from the inline-start and
mirrors under `[dir="rtl"]`, as the header's 2 px bar already does in `globals.css`; positions use
logical properties (`inset-inline`, `inset-block-end`), never `left` or `right`. A long German or
Polish name wraps as today (clause 1 (d), *Reading*). `/ar-XB` is the check.

**Compliance.** Accessibility (WCAG 2.2 AA): the motion is decorative and never needed to use the
page; `prefers-reduced-motion` removes every movement (2.3.3, met beyond AA); nothing flashes
(2.3.1); the focus ring is unchanged (2.4.7); the two new elements are `aria-hidden` and empty, so
the card link's accessible name is unchanged. The pill's text keeps its contrast at both ends of
the fade, because the layer's colour is today's hover colour and both pairs are already in
`src/modules/ui/tokens/contrast.ts`. The card's price, its inclusive text and the example label are
unchanged (price shown = price charged). No data flow changes; the RoPA is unchanged.

**AC-44 amended.** It gains, on the listing T-48's `hoveredCard` opens (`SHOP` in
`tests/e2e/motion.spec.ts`) at 1440 × 900 with a fine pointer, and on the same page under `/ar-XB`
where named:
- `--duration-hover` is declared as exactly `500ms` in `src/app/globals.css` and in
  `docs/design/system/tokens.css`, and every rule in the shipped CSS that uses it has a selector
  containing `[data-fo-card-motion]`;
- **at rest**, the first moving card's link and photograph have computed `transform` `none`, its
  shadow layer's `opacity` is `0` and its `box-shadow` is `--shadow-letter`'s computed value, and
  its line's `transform` is `matrix(0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0)`;
- **hovered, once its transitions have ended:** the link's `transform` is
  `matrix(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, -4)`, the photograph's is `matrix(1.06, 0, 0, 1.06, 0, 0)`, the shadow
  layer's `opacity` is `1`, the line's `transform` is `matrix(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0)`, and the heading's
  `text-decoration-line` is `none`;
- the link, the photograph and the shadow layer report `transition-duration` `0.5s` and
  `transition-timing-function` `cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.7, 0.2, 1)`; the line reports `0.32s` and
  `cubic-bezier(0.3, 0, 0, 1)`; the shadow layer's `transition-property` is `opacity` and the
  others' is `transform`;
- the line's `transform-origin` x component is `0px` on the `en-gb` page and equals the line's
  width on `/ar-XB`;
- **the priority card** has no `data-fo-card-motion`, no shadow layer and no line element, and its
  heading's `text-decoration-line` is `underline` while hovered;
- **a `primary` `Button`** (the first one on a built page that renders one; the test names the
  page and asserts the element is that variant), hovered: its layer's `opacity` is `1`, with
  `transition-duration` `0.2s` and `cubic-bezier(0.2, 0, 0.13, 1)`, and its `background-color`
  equals its rest value; held pressed: its `transform` is `matrix(0.98, 0, 0, 0.98, 0, 0)` with
  `transition-duration` `0.12s` and `cubic-bezier(0.2, 0, 0.13, 1)`; released: its
  `transition-duration` is `0.2s` with `cubic-bezier(0.3, 0, 0, 1)`, and its `transform` returns to
  `none`;
- **coarse pointer** (the mobile project): a tapped moving card's link `transform` stays `none` and
  its shadow layer's `opacity` stays `0`;
- **reduced motion emulated:** a hovered moving card's link and photograph `transform` stay
  `none`, its shadow layer's `opacity` stays `0`, its line's `transform` is
  `matrix(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0)` at rest and while hovered and its `opacity` is `1` in the first frame
  after the hover; a held press leaves the pill's `transform` `none`;
- CLS is 0 across a card's hover-in and hover-out.

**T-48 amended.** It gains:
- **Unit** (`tests/unit/motion-css.test.ts`, over the shipped stylesheet): the `--duration-hover`
  declaration in both files, value for value; each rule of clause 1, read by selector, property and
  value (the two transforms, the two opacities, the durations, the curves, the line's two
  `transform-origin` values, the press's two durations), each inside
  `(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)` and `prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference` where clause 5
  puts it; `--duration-hover` used only under `[data-fo-card-motion]`; no `will-change` in a rule
  that applies at rest. Unit (`tests/unit/motion-markup.test.tsx`): a moving card renders exactly
  one shadow layer and one line element, both `aria-hidden`; a priority card renders neither and no
  `data-fo-card-motion`.
- **E2e** (`tests/e2e/motion.spec.ts`): every AC-44 bullet above. A hovered value is read after the
  link's `transitionend`, or after at least 600 ms; a fixed wait under 500 ms reads a
  mid-transition value and is not allowed.
- **Red cases, each a mutation of the shipped CSS or markup that must turn a named case red:** the
  lift back to `-2px`; the zoom back to `1.03`; `--duration-hover` at `400ms`, or declared in one
  file only; the card's curve set to `--ease-standard`; a `box-shadow` transition in place of the
  layer's opacity; the line's origin at `100% 50%` without `dir="rtl"`; the line at `--duration-base`;
  the press release at `--duration-fast`; the pill's hover back to a `background-color` change;
  `--duration-hover` used on `[data-fo-consent-sheet="fixed"]`; the shadow layer rendered on the
  priority card; the card rules moved out of `(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)` (the mobile case
  lifts); the line's resting `scaleX(1)` dropped under reduce (its transform changes on hover). The
  existing reds (a fade-in on the hero image, a `height` keyframe, a 200 ms transition on the
  priority card) stay.

**AC-6 and T-6** (as A24 clause 9 (c) reads them): text unchanged; clause 5's reduced-motion
bullet is how this amendment meets them.

**Owner.** TASK-277 (its row and brief are on PR 281; the row is already widened to attach motion 6
to every product card and to the pill press) builds these values in the same PR: the token in both
files, the two card elements, the CSS of clause 1 and the T-48 gains. It starts after PR 282 merges
(clause 6). *Reading on the class:* the diff is styles, component markup and their tests, but it
edits the guard on the LCP card (AC-44), so the PR keeps the breaker; the orchestrator names the
class in the work order.

Open for the founder: none blocking. (i) The consent banner's and the checkout's pills keep today's
hover and press; default: unchanged until the founder asks for them to match (a new row). (ii) The
*Reading* of clause 1 (d), one line under a wrapped name, can be overruled in one word.

**A-founder-2026-10-19f (pointer): The 500 document and the language hints read a small client
module, so the locale rows stay out of browser JavaScript (§13 Q13 option (a); §14 A1; AC-24 and
AC-25; spec 003 §14 A-founder-2026-10-19f owns the rule, its new AC-52 and T-52; no new AC or T id
here).**
Status: approved in substance by the founder's ruling of 2026-10-19, verbatim: "go with the premium
proposed version and fix c". Its "fix c" is recorded in spec 003 §14 A-founder-2026-10-19f; its
"go with the premium proposed version" is A-founder-2026-10-19e's. The founder confirms this text
when they approve the docs PR that carries it.

- **Q13 option (a), restated.** The two zod-free client paths (`src/modules/i18n/error-document.ts`
  for `global-error.tsx` and `[locale]/error.tsx`, and `src/modules/i18n/hints.ts` for the language
  popup island) stay zod-free and now read an import-free client module that mirrors the x-default
  locale's `code`, `bcp47` and `dir` and the launch codes. They no longer import
  `src/config/locales.data.ts`, whose rows (path segments included) stay server-side.
- **A1's budget is not raised.** PR 270 (TASK-182) measured 131 207 B against 131 072 B
  (`resource-summary.script.size`, CI run 39211395643); this fix, inside PR 270, brings it back
  under, measured in CI as spec 003 AC-52 (c) and (d) say.
- **AC-25 gains:** "no client chunk a browser fetches for an audited document carries a locale
  row's path segments (spec 003 AC-52 (c))". **T-27 gains** spec 003 T-52's build-output case.
- **SEO, i18n, compliance:** spec 003 §14 A-founder-2026-10-19f's notes. No URL, key or data flow
  change.
- **Owner.** TASK-182, in PR 270 (spec 003 §14 A-founder-2026-10-19f, Owner).


**A-founder-2026-10-20a (pointer): the header follows the destination in the URL; no eyebrow repeats the trail; the home says "Ordering opens soon" in its first screen; motions 11 and 12 never hide content in print (spec 043 §5.3 A, D, F, H; spec 043 AC-1 to AC-4, AC-6, AC-8, AC-15, AC-18; no new AC or T id here).**
Status: draft, carried by spec 043's docs PR. Binding when the founder approves spec 043 (its §13 Q1 picks the header model; default A, as below). The founder's words that started it, 2026-10-20, in chat, verbatim: "the website seems a bit confusing, so a few pages and everything. So fix that up."

- **The category row (§14 A20; spec 008 §14 A14 (a) to (c)).** On a page whose URL names a destination with a shop root in that locale (shop root, country category, country occasion, product page, that destination's guide), the row's Our selection, Bouquets, Roses, Plants, Birthday and Sympathy, on the desktop row and in the phone Menu (AC-47), link to that destination's pages as spec 043 §5.2 `destinationShopLinks()` says. Elsewhere the row is unchanged. The row is resolved per route with no request header, cookie or client path read (the `@notice` / `@switcher` slot precedent); the document stays one cache entry (AC-14's zero non-200 links, no `Vary`, no `Set-Cookie` unchanged). Spec 043 AC-1 to AC-4 and AC-6, T-1 to T-4 and T-6.
- **Eyebrows.** No eyebrow on a listing or hub page type equals a label of that page's breadcrumb (spec 043 AC-18, T-18). The occasion hub's "Occasions" eyebrow goes; the country listings' state eyebrow goes by spec 008 §14 A-founder-2026-10-20a.
- **The home's first screen (A21 clause 4).** While `anyDeliveryDatesOpen()` is false, the not-open state text (spec 007 §14 A-founder-2026-10-14c's words) is one line in the sentence section, inside the first viewport at 1440 × 900 and 390 × 844 (spec 043 AC-8, T-8). The home's product row heading reads "Our *selection*" and the basis line `home.trending.basis` is deleted (spec 043 AC-10; this supersedes the design README's "'Popular choices' with an honest basis line" for the home row once the founder approves the copy).
- **Motions 11 and 12 (A23 clause 11, A24 clause 9).** Their `opacity: 0` start states apply on screen only; under `@media print` every element they hold is at full opacity (spec 043 AC-15, T-15). Values, timings and reduced-motion rules are unchanged (spec 042 owns any change to motion 11's look).
- **SEO, i18n, compliance:** spec 043 §6, §7, §8. No URL, canonical, sitemap, hreflang or schema change; no new key.
- **Owner:** spec 043 §12 tasks 1, 3 and 4.
**A-founder-2026-10-20a: The photo-to-page morph is the one exception to A23 clause 11's LCP
rule, on a card click from `lg` up only, transform-only (spec 042 §5.3.1 and §13 Q1; A23 clause 11;
AC-44 and T-48 amended; no new AC or T id).**
Status: approved by the founder's answer of 2026-10-20 to spec 042 §13, verbatim: "all defaults,
keep going" (relayed by the orchestrator). Q1's default is (a) with transform-only keyframes; spec
042 applies the advisor's fix F2 (`docs/advice/2026-10-20-spec-042.md`), which confines it to `lg`
and up.

**Original.** A23 clause 11: "The LCP element is painted at its final position and opacity in the
first frame it renders; no entrance animation, fade-in or delay applies to it." AC-44's second
bullet asserts it on the AC-41 page set and the product page.

**Corrected.**
1. **The exception.** On the product page, the hero photograph (its LCP element) may carry the
   product's `view-transition-name` and be shown through the view transition's group for at most
   `--duration-slow` 320 ms, `--ease-emphasised`, once, **only** when all of these hold: the
   navigation is a same-origin document navigation started by activating a product card's link;
   the viewport is `lg` or wider; `prefers-reduced-motion` is `no-preference`; the browser supports
   cross-document view transitions. Both documents opt in with `@view-transition` inside both media
   conditions.
2. **Transform-only.** The group's animation is custom keyframes that change only `transform`
   (and `opacity` where needed), replacing the UA's default group keyframes, which animate `width`
   and `height`. AC-44's keyframes bullet therefore holds with no exception.
3. **Never on a first paint.** A first load, a reload, a JavaScript-off load, an entry from another
   site, any navigation not started from a card, and every viewport below `lg` keep clause 11
   exactly as written: no view transition and the hero at its final position and opacity in its
   first frame.
4. **The one script.** The name is set on the activated card's photograph only, in a `pageswap`
   handler of at most 400 B Brotli in the deferred motion module (A-founder-2026-10-15b), never as a
   static per-card name, because one product can render twice on a page (the home rails) and two
   elements with one name abort the transition. This is within clause 11's "toggling a class on a
   client transition" allowance; a click before the module loads navigates as today.
5. **Measured.** Spec 042 AC-7, AC-8 and AC-9, with their tests there, carry the proof,
   including the LCP medians with the morph on and off within 50 ms. Phones get the morph only by a
   later amendment, after spec 023's RUM shows its mobile LCP cost.

**AC-44 amended.** Its LCP bullet is asserted on a first load, a reload, a JavaScript-off load, a
non-card navigation and a card navigation below `lg`, exactly as written. On a card navigation at
`lg` and up, spec 042 AC-7 and AC-9 govern the hero for the transition's duration; after it ends
the hero's computed `opacity` is 1 and its `transform` is `none`. **T-48 amended.** Its LCP case
gains the card navigation at 390 x 844 (no transition, the bullet holds); red with the `lg`
condition removed from the `@view-transition` rule.

**SEO.** Lab LCP cannot see the morph (Lighthouse loads cold); field LCP on desktop product pages
reached from a card is the one exposure, about 5 ms in the third-party RUM test the advisor cites,
and spec 042 AC-9 bounds it at 50 ms in CI. Phones are not exposed. No
URL, HTML text, link, canonical, robots, hreflang, sitemap or schema changes; rendering stays as
`plan/01` §3 sets it. **i18n.** No key; a mirrored layout morphs mirrored. **Compliance.** WCAG
2.3.3 is met by the reduced-motion condition; no data flow; RoPA unchanged.

**Owner.** Spec 042's task 042-1, after TASK-244 merges. It keeps the breaker (the LCP guard).

**A-founder-2026-10-20b: `--duration-hover` may also time spec 042's second-photo hover layer
(spec 042 §5.3.3 and §13 Q5; amends A-founder-2026-10-19e clause 4; no new AC or T id).**
Status: approved by the founder's answer of 2026-10-20 to spec 042 §13, verbatim: "all defaults,
keep going" (relayed by the orchestrator). Q5's default is to amend clause 4.

**Original.** A-founder-2026-10-19e clause 4 (merged to `main` by PR 283) lets `--duration-hover`
time only the hover-in and hover-out of a moving card's link, its photograph and its shadow layer.

**Corrected.** Clause 4's list gains a fourth element: spec 042's `aria-hidden` second-photo hover
layer inside a moving card's clipped photograph box. Its `opacity` fades over `--duration-hover`
500 ms with `--ease-photo`, so it moves with the zoom. Its rule's selector contains
`[data-fo-card-motion]`, so A-founder-2026-10-19e's AC-44 gain ("every rule that uses it has a
selector containing `[data-fo-card-motion]`") holds unchanged. Everything else in clause 4 stands:
never the name's line, a pill, anything without a pointer's hover, anything at first paint, or the
LCP element and its ancestors. The priority card renders no layer.

**Tests.** Spec 042 AC-12 and its test there carry the layer's values. T-48's unit scan of
`--duration-hover` uses (A-founder-2026-10-19e) passes over the layer's rule without change.
**SEO, i18n, compliance.** As A-founder-2026-10-19e's notes: hover only, CSS only, no key, no data
flow. **Owner.** Spec 042's task 042-3.

**A-founder-2026-10-20c: Caveat also renders the card preview at checkout step 2 (spec 042 §5.3.2
and §13 Q4 (a); amends A21 clause 3; T-5 amended; no new AC or T id).**
Status: approved by the founder's answer of 2026-10-20 to spec 042 §13, verbatim: "all defaults,
keep going" (relayed by the orchestrator). Q4's default is (a). The advisor proposed (b), the
display face on both; the founder chose the default on 2026-10-20.

**Original.** A21 clause 3: Caveat is "the buyer's own words in the printed-card preview only;
never a price, a button, a label or a heading, and never on another page", and "Caveat loads only
on the product page", with "≤30 KB for Caveat on a product page".

**Corrected.**
1. **Reach.** Caveat sets the buyer's own words in the printed-card preview on two pages: the
   product page and checkout step 2 (spec 010), where spec 042's one shared preview component
   renders. Never a price, a button, a label, a heading or any other page.
2. **Loading and budget.** Caveat loads on those two pages only and is never preloaded. The
   ≤30 KB allowance for Caveat applies on checkout step 2 as on the product page; every other
   ceiling of clause 3 is unchanged and is not raised.
3. **The word.** The preview is "the card preview", never "handwritten"; its caption stays each
   page's existing printed label (A21 clause 5).

**T-5 amended.** "On non-PDP pages no Caveat request" reads "on pages other than the product
page and checkout step 2, no Caveat request"; red with Caveat requested on checkout step 3.

**SEO.** Checkout is `noindex` and server-rendered `no-store` (spec 010 §5.4); no indexable page
gains a font byte, and the product page is unchanged. **i18n.** No key; a script Caveat lacks falls
back to the body face (spec 042 §7). **Compliance.** The preview must show what is printed: the
florist's print instructions (spec 011) name the face, which spec 011 records in its own amendment;
contrast stays in `tokens/contrast.ts` (WCAG 1.4.3). No data flow; RoPA unchanged.

**Owner.** Spec 042's task 042-2. It keeps the breaker (checkout, font budget).

### A-founder-2026-10-23: the script budget gains 1 KiB while Next 16.4.2 lands

**Status: approved (founder, 2026-10-23, "A").** The security bump TASK-375 (next 16.3.8 to 16.4.2, @sentry/nextjs to ^10.75.3, critical advisories GHSA-2464-fhr4-w9xw, GHSA-p3m5-59mp-jv5r, GHSA-mchm-gjx6-7h55) measures 131 428 B of Brotli script on all 16 Lighthouse URLs against the 131 072 B budget of §13 Q13 and AC-24: 356 B over, all of it from Next 16.4's own runtime (main on 16.3.8: 130 316 B). The founder chose option A of three (A: raise the budget; B: trim app script first, which cannot start until the bump merges; C: re-enable the shared runtime and hash its inline script).

- The script budget is **132 096 B (129 KiB)** for every URL that is not a product address, and **134 144 B** for product addresses (the product allowance stays budget + 2 KiB, §14 product-page rule). `CLIENT_JS_BUDGET_BYTES` in `scripts/client-js-budget.ts`, the two `resource-summary:script:size` assertions in `lighthouserc.json` and their unit pins move together in TASK-375.
- **The raise is temporary.** TASK-376 trims the script back under 131 072 B and restores the original budget; the 1 KiB is headroom for the bump only, never for new features.
- Nothing else in AC-24 changes: Brotli transfer, all 16 URLs, the same measurement.

**A-founder-2026-10-21d: The home sentence's three choices open our own listbox on a mouse (A21 clause 4; A24 clause 9, motion 10; A-founder-2026-10-15b; AC-6, AC-24 and AC-55 extended; new AC-60 to AC-71, T-65 to T-77).**
Status: approved 2026-10-21 on the founder's choice "Our own dropdown on desktop. (Recommended)"; §13 Q1 and Q2 on their defaults (scope stays the home sentence; a missed script budget is escalated, not traded). The founder's words, 2026-10-21, in chat with a screenshot of the home sentence "send flowers to [who] in [country] for [occasion]", verbatim: "also this dropdown here seems weird... it should also follow the design and animation motion whatever idk". Asked native or ours, the founder chose, verbatim: "Our own dropdown on desktop. (Recommended)". The founder confirms this text when they approve the docs PR that carries it. Class of the work: **review-only** (presentation; no money, orders, payments, dates, auth, migration or legal text). The orchestrator names the class in the work order.

**Original.** `src/modules/ui/home/SentencePicker.tsx` renders the three choices as native `<select>` elements, skinned (A21 clause 4, A24): the display face, a dashed underline, a drawn chevron. When opened, the browser draws its own list in the operating system's font, surface and corners, with no motion of ours. `SentenceIsland.tsx` only swaps the occasion labels' possessive when "who" changes.

**Corrected.**
1. **When.** On a primary pointer that is a mouse or trackpad, `(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)`, each of the three controls (who `#send-who`, country `#send-country`, occasion `#send-occasion`) opens our listbox. Where that query does not match (phones, tablets, any coarse pointer) the native `<select>` stays, unchanged: the operating system's picker is the better control on a touch screen. The query is read on load and re-read when it changes (a tablet docked to a mouse): the enhancement is built or torn down, never half-built.
2. **The server HTML does not change.** The response still holds exactly the three `<select>` elements, every `<option>` with its text (the seven destinations, six occasions in their default possessive form, seven "who" values), the `data-label-*` attributes and the form that submits `country` and `occasion` to `/api/send/{locale}`. It holds no `role="listbox"`, no trigger button and no new attribute. With JavaScript off the page works exactly as today. Nothing indexable depends on JavaScript (plan/01 §3: the home stays as rendered today).
3. **The native select stays in the form and in the layout.** After the enhancement attaches, each `<select>` stays in the flow with its box unchanged (so the sentence does not reflow and the layout shift is zero by construction), is set to `visibility: hidden` (out of the tab order and the accessibility tree), and remains the source of truth: it is what the form submits and what the pronoun sync reads. Our trigger `<button type="button">` is laid over the select's box (`inset: 0`, logical properties only) inside the same `data-fo-pick` span, so the chevron, the dashed underline and the one-time shimmer (motion 4) are unchanged.
4. **The trigger.** Same type, size, colour, underline and ink room as today's select (TASK-231: the last glyph of an italic never clips). It shows the selected option's text, which **replaces the word in the sentence**. It carries `aria-haspopup="listbox"`, `aria-expanded` (`false`/`true`), `aria-controls` the listbox's id, and an accessible name made of the field's existing label followed by the selected text: who is named by the letter's heading (`home.sentence.heading`, as the select is today), country by `finder.country.label`, occasion by `corridor.calendar.occasion`. **No new message key.** Its box is at least 44 px high (§5.3).
5. **The listbox.** One panel per control, `role="listbox"` named by the same label (`aria-labelledby`), holding one `role="option"` per `<option>`, in the select's order, with the select's current option `aria-selected="true"` and every other `aria-selected="false"`. A destination that is not yet open (the select's `disabled` options, labelled "not yet" in words) is `aria-disabled="true"`: reachable by arrow keys so a screen reader announces it, never selectable. Roving focus: while open, exactly one option has `tabindex="0"`, the rest `-1`, and the focused option is `document.activeElement`. Opening focuses the selected option (the first enabled one if none). Each option is at least 44 px high. The panel is at least as wide as its trigger, scrolls inside itself when taller than the room, and opens upward when there is less room below than its height. Opening one closes any other.
6. **Keyboard.** On the trigger: `Enter`, `Space`, `ArrowDown` and `ArrowUp` open. In the open listbox: `ArrowDown` and `ArrowUp` move one option and do not wrap; `Home` and `End` go to the first and last; **type-ahead** (printable keys, a 500 ms buffer, case-insensitive prefix match using `Intl.Collator(locale, { sensitivity: "base" })` of the page's locale, repeating one letter cycles through matches); `Enter` and `Space` on an enabled option select it, close the listbox and return focus to the trigger; on a disabled option they do nothing and the listbox stays open; `Escape` closes, returns focus to the trigger and leaves the value alone; `Tab` closes without selecting and lets focus move on from the trigger. Arrow, `Space` and `Home`/`End` never scroll the page while the listbox is open, and `Enter` never submits the form from inside it.
7. **Pointer.** A click on the trigger toggles. A click on an enabled option selects it, closes and returns focus to the trigger; pointer hover moves the highlight only, never the selection. A click outside the panel and trigger, a scroll of the page, a window resize and `pagehide` close the listbox without changing the value and without moving focus to the trigger.
8. **Value flow.** Choosing writes the value to the native `<select>` and dispatches `input` then `change` on it (once), so the pronoun sync in `SentenceIsland` runs unchanged and the form submits what the trigger shows. When "who" changes, the occasion trigger's text is re-read from the select's current option text, so picking "dad" turns "her birthday" into "his birthday" in the sentence with the occasion listbox closed. On mount the trigger reads the select's value (a back/forward restore that kept a different value shows it). The "who" select keeps **no `name`**: a relationship is never submitted, stored, logged or sent (plan/07). The enhancement adds no cookie, no storage key, no network request and no analytics event.
9. **Motion 10 (A24 clause 9).** The panel opens with a fade and an 8 px rise, `opacity` 0 to 1 and `translateY(8px)` to `none`, over `--duration-base` (200 ms) with `--ease-standard`, exactly the language popup's values (`@starting-style`). It closes with an opacity-only fade over `--duration-fast` (120 ms). Under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` both directions are an opacity-only fade of `--duration-fast` and **no movement**: the panel's `transform` is `none` throughout. Its selector joins the reduced-motion rule in `src/app/globals.css` beside the language popup, the Menu panel and the consent sheet. Only `opacity` and `transform` ever change over time (AC-44's rule: no `width`, `height`, `top`, `left` or `max-height` animation). The chevron does not animate.
10. **Surface, type, not clipped.** The panel uses the design system and no literal: the card surface `--color-surface-card`, the radius `--radius-letter` (the radius of the card the sentence sits in), the shadow `--shadow-md` (the language popup's), a 1 px border token so the panel still has an edge in forced-colours mode; options in the sentence's own type family (never the browser's), the highlighted option on `--color-blush` (the select's hover colour today), the selected option marked by a drawn check mark as well as colour, a disabled option in `--color-ink-subtle` with its "not yet" words. Every token pair stays inside `src/modules/ui/tokens/contrast.ts` (AC-3, 4.5:1). The desktop hero card has `overflow: hidden` (`HomeHero.tsx`), so the panel **must escape it**: top layer (the `popover` attribute) or a portal to `body`, positioned from the trigger's rectangle with logical offsets. The implementer chooses and records it in the brief. The artboard (clause 14) may substitute another token of the same system for the three named above; the ACs then read the artboard's token.
11. **Loading (A-founder-2026-10-15b).** The enhancement is a client chunk in the **deferred set** that `TASK-244` declares in `src/modules/ui` (its `AfterPaint` mechanism): it is neither requested nor finished before the LCP element has painted, and the first paint is the native select exactly as today. It makes no layout (clause 3). If a select has focus when the chunk is ready, the enhancement for that control waits until it blurs, so nothing is taken from a reader mid-choice. A click or key on a control before the chunk loads is honoured by the native select. Target: the new chunk is **at most 3,072 B Brotli** and the A1 budget (131,072 B on locale documents, A-founder-2026-10-18a's figure on the product page) is **not raised**; if the home cannot take it, the task escalates with the measured number (§13 Q2 below).
12. **Scope.** The sentence on the home only. The component lives under `src/modules/ui/home/` (a client island and a pure model for the option list and type-ahead); nothing else on the site changes (§13 Q1 below). What the sentence says, which fields it submits and where `sentenceTarget()` routes are untouched.
13. **Existing tests that drive these selects** (`tests/e2e/home.spec.ts` L369 and L435 to L465, `tests/e2e/home-round2.spec.ts` L63, `tests/a11y/home.spec.ts` L72 to L80) run on a fine-pointer project and would wait forever on a hidden select. They move to one helper, `chooseSentence(page, field, value)` in `tests/support/`, which drives the listbox when the trigger exists and the native `selectOption` when it does not (the `e2e-mobile` project and JavaScript-off contexts). No assertion in them is weakened: each still asserts the same URL, text and attribute.
14. **Design first.** This amendment changes a control, so `/design` runs before `/plan-tasks` (CLAUDE.md): a "Sentence listbox" artboard in `docs/design/system/components.dc.html` (closed, open, option hover, selected, not-yet, opened upward, long list scrolling, `dir="rtl"`) and the open state on `docs/design/wireframes/home-desktop.dc.html`, plus the open/close row in `docs/design/system/motion.dc.html`, with `/dev/components` gaining the same states (A3). Implementers match them pixel for pixel; where an artboard and this text differ on a token or a size, the artboard wins and the brief records it.

**SEO.** Priority 1. The server-rendered HTML of the home and of every page that embeds the sentence is unchanged, so crawl, indexing, canonical, hreflang, sitemap, structured data and every internal link are unchanged (AC-60). The listbox is created after the LCP paint, off the paint path (AC-69), so LCP is not touched and A18's measurement basis stands. CLS stays below 0.05 and the sentence's shift is zero (AC-70). The country names stay in the HTML as `<option>` text as today.
**i18n.** No new key (clause 4). Option text, the possessive and the "not yet" words come from the select's existing messages in every locale. Type-ahead matches with the page locale's collator, so `/pl` finds "Polska" on "po" and `/de` finds "Niederlande" on "n". Positions, offsets and the check mark use logical properties and the mirrored icon set, so `dir="rtl"` mirrors with no code change (no RTL locale ships; the pseudo-RTL project covers it, AC-67).
**Compliance.** WCAG 2.2: 1.4.3 contrast (tokens), 2.1.1 and 2.1.2 keyboard with no trap (Escape, Tab), 2.4.3 focus order, 2.4.7 visible focus, 2.5.8 target size (44 px), 2.3.3 reduced motion, 4.1.2 name/role/value (the ARIA listbox pattern). No data flow: the relationship chosen in "who" never leaves the page (clause 8, AC-65); no cookie, storage or tracking is added, so consent and the RoPA are unchanged. No price is shown in the control (price-display rules untouched). The CSP is unchanged: no inline `<script>` and no `style` attribute in the server HTML; positioning is set through the CSSOM.

**Acceptance criteria.**
- **AC-60** On `/en`, `/de` and `/pl` with JavaScript disabled: the sentence form contains exactly three `<select>` elements (`#send-who`, `#send-country`, `#send-occasion`); the country select has exactly 7 `<option>` elements and the occasion select exactly 6; the `name` attributes on the form's controls are exactly `country` and `occasion`; the HTML response contains zero elements with `role="listbox"`, zero with `aria-haspopup`, and zero trigger buttons (`[data-fo-sentence-trigger]`); choosing Poland and Birthday and activating Continue navigates to the URL `sentenceTarget()` returns for them, the same URL as before this amendment.
- **AC-61** In a fine-pointer context after the chunk has loaded, each of the three controls has exactly one `button[aria-haspopup="listbox"][aria-expanded="false"]` and its native select has computed `visibility: hidden`; in a coarse-pointer context (the `e2e-mobile` project) there are zero such buttons, the three selects are visible and `selectOption("PL")` works on the country select; with the media query mocked to change from fine to coarse, the buttons are removed and the selects become visible again, and back to fine rebuilds them with exactly one button per control (never two).
- **AC-62** Closed: the trigger's `aria-controls` equals its listbox's `id`; its accessible name equals the field's label text followed by the selected option's text (for example, in `/en`, the `finder.country.label` text then "Poland"). Open: the listbox has `role="listbox"` and an accessible name equal to the field's label; its `[role="option"]` count equals the select's `<option>` count; exactly one option has `aria-selected="true"` and it is the select's current value; each not-yet destination has `aria-disabled="true"`; exactly one option has `tabindex="0"`, the others `-1`, and it is `document.activeElement`.
- **AC-63** With the keyboard only on `/en`: `Enter`, `Space`, `ArrowDown` and `ArrowUp` on the country trigger each open it with focus on the selected option; `ArrowDown` from the last option leaves focus on the last option (no wrap); `Home` focuses the first option and `End` the last; typing `n` focuses "Netherlands" and typing `po` within 500 ms focuses "Poland"; on `/pl`, typing `po` focuses "Polska"; `Enter` on an enabled option closes the listbox, sets the select's value to that option's value, makes the trigger's text equal the option's text, returns focus to the trigger, fires `change` on the select exactly once and does not navigate; `Space` does the same; `Enter` on a not-yet destination leaves the listbox open and the value unchanged; `Escape` closes with the value unchanged and focus on the trigger; `Tab` closes with the value unchanged and `document.activeElement` outside the listbox; `window.scrollY` is unchanged by `ArrowDown`, `Space` and `End` while open.
- **AC-64** With the pointer: a click on the trigger sets `aria-expanded="true"`, a second click sets it `false`; a click on an enabled option selects it, closes and focuses the trigger; moving the pointer over another option leaves `aria-selected` and the select's value unchanged; opening the country listbox while the who listbox is open leaves exactly one listbox open; a click on the page body outside both, `window.scrollBy`, and a viewport resize each set `aria-expanded="false"` with the value unchanged.
- **AC-65** After choosing "dad" in the who listbox, with no listbox open, the occasion trigger's text is the select's current option text and equals the `his` form ("his birthday" in `/en`), and the `#send-who` select still has no `name` attribute; choosing Poland and Sympathy and activating Continue reaches the same URL the native path reaches (`tests/e2e/home.spec.ts` L369); the submitted URL's query contains only `country` and `occasion`; across the whole interaction `localStorage`, `sessionStorage` and `document.cookie` hold exactly the keys they held before it, and the page makes zero requests; after a back/forward restore the trigger's text equals the restored select's selected option text.
- **AC-66** In a no-preference context, opening a listbox gives its panel exactly two `CSSTransition`s, on `opacity` and `transform`, each with a duration of 200 ms and the timing function of `--ease-standard`; closing gives exactly one, on `opacity`, 120 ms, none on `transform`. In a `reduce` context, opening gives exactly one, on `opacity`, 120 ms, and the panel's computed `transform` is `none` in every frame sampled. Across both contexts no animation on the panel targets any property other than `opacity` or `transform`. `src/app/globals.css` contains a `@starting-style` rule for the panel with `opacity: 0` and `transform: translateY(8px)`, and its selector appears in the reduced-motion rule beside the language popup.
- **AC-67** At 1440 x 900 and 1280 x 720, with the country listbox open: the panel's computed `background-color` equals the resolved `--color-surface-card`, its `border-radius` equals the resolved `--radius-letter`, its `box-shadow` equals the resolved `--shadow-md` and its border-width is at least 1 px; no `border-radius`, `font-family`, `color` or `background` in the new CSS is a literal (a unit scan); the first family of an option's `font-family` equals the sentence's (`[data-fo-sentence] p`) first family and is not a system family; the panel's bounding box lies inside the viewport, and `document.elementFromPoint` at the centre of every option returns that option (the hero card's `overflow: hidden` does not clip it); every option's height is at least 44 px and the panel's width is at least the trigger's; with the trigger scrolled to 100 px above the viewport's bottom edge the panel's box still lies inside the viewport; under `PSEUDO_RTL_PROJECT` the panel's inline-start edge aligns with the trigger's inline-start edge.
- **AC-68** `axe-core` over `/en` with the country listbox open, with the who listbox open, and closed, reports zero violations of impact serious or critical, and none of `aria-hidden-focus`, `aria-valid-attr-value`, `aria-required-children`, `nested-interactive`; the focused option and the focused trigger each have a computed `outline-style` other than `none` and an outline-width of at least 2 px in the focus colour.
- **AC-69** On `/en`, `/en-gb`, `/de` and `/pl` under Lighthouse's mobile emulation: the enhancement's chunk is in the deferred set and its resource-timing `startTime` is not before the largest-contentful-paint entry's render time (AC-55 extended); its Brotli transfer size is at most 3,072 B; the script budget (A1) and the `lighthouse` job's LCP assertion are unchanged and green. With that chunk's request blocked, a click on the country select opens the native control and `selectOption("PL")` succeeds; with a select focused when the chunk arrives, that control is not replaced until it blurs.
- **AC-70** At 1440 x 900 and 1280 x 720 with JavaScript on, the layout shifts recorded inside `[data-fo-sentence]` (`shiftInside` in `tests/support/layout-shift.ts`) from load through enhancement, open, select and close sum to 0, and the page's CLS is below 0.05; just before enhancement and just after it, the trigger's `getBoundingClientRect()` x, y, width and height equal the select's to within 0.5 px, and after picking a different country the trigger's width again equals the select's; the trigger's text span has `scrollWidth <= clientWidth` for "Poland" (no clipped glyph); while a listbox is open the Continue button's `getBoundingClientRect().top` is unchanged.
- **AC-71** Visual baselines (Linux, from CI, per the visual runbook) at 1440 x 900: `home-sentence-closed-1440` (the sentence region, every listbox closed) and `home-sentence-open-1440` (country listbox open, Poland selected and focused), within the project's existing threshold; the existing `/en` 1440 home baseline passes **unchanged**, which proves the closed trigger matches the select it covers; the existing 390 home baseline passes unchanged (native select on a phone).

Red when a trigger button or a `role="listbox"` is rendered on the server, or when a `<select>` is dropped | AC-60 |
| T-66 | unit (jsdom, `tests/unit/`, with a mocked `matchMedia`) | The controller builds one trigger per control when the query matches, none when it does not, removes them and unhides the selects on a change to coarse, rebuilds exactly one on a change back, and waits on a focused select. Red when the query is ignored, when a change builds a second trigger, or when a focused select is replaced | AC-61, AC-69 |

Red when the 500 ms window is 5 s, or when disabled options become selectable | AC-62, AC-63 |

**Owner and order.** `/design` first (clause 14), then `/plan-tasks` adds one task, which depends on `TASK-244` (the deferred-set mechanism) and on the A-founder-2026-10-19e motion work being merged. It owns `src/modules/ui/home/SentenceIsland.tsx` (or a sibling island), a new pure model beside `sentence-model.ts`, the CSS in `src/app/globals.css`, the deferred-set entry in `src/modules/ui`, `tests/support/` (`chooseSentence`) and the moved cases of clause 13, the gallery states and the baselines. Review-only. Renumber the new ids if another amendment has taken AC-60 or T-65 when this merges, and run `node scripts/specs-index.ts` after the insertion.

Open for the founder (defaults bind until answered):
- **Q1: Do the site's other native selects (the listing "Sort by", checkout and account fields) get the same listbox?** Default: no. This amendment is the sentence only (clause 12). If the founder wants it everywhere, the component moves to `src/modules/ui/primitives/` as a new row against the listing and checkout specs, not by widening this task.
- **Q2: If the home cannot fit 3,072 B inside the script budget, what gives?** Default: the task escalates with the measured number, the budget is not raised (A1), and the fallback is loading the enhancement on first pointer movement over the sentence (after the LCP, so the native select serves the first interaction) instead of on idle. The founder can instead say "raise the budget by that amount".
